# GitHub Security Inspector

Portable contract for GitHub organization, repository, Actions, code-security, webhook, key, and App-installation assessments.

## Purpose

Assess GitHub organization, repository, Actions, code-security, webhook, deploy-key, and App-installation posture through read-only REST and GraphQL APIs.

## Design guidance

Compute effective repository controls across all policy layers, preserve per-repository and connection completeness, and withhold private keys, token material, webhook secrets, and untrusted error bodies at every sink.

## Shared integration contract

This specification requires [shared integration contract version 1.1](./integration-contract.md). The raw contract is available at https://raw.githubusercontent.com/ethanolivertroy/grclanker/main/specs/integration-contract.md.

## Known runtime gaps

- REST Link pagination and GraphQL connection pagination retain independent totals, pageInfo, repeated-cursor, null-cursor, cap, and per-repository failure states.
- Effective branch protection merges organization rulesets, repository rulesets, branch rules, and legacy protection without treating an unreadable layer as absent.
- GitHub App private keys and API error bodies are reduced to fixed safe error envelopes before in-memory evidence, formatted output, bundle files, or archives are produced.
- Audit streaming, repository security-policy presence, package visibility, interactive OAuth, and organization-wide alert enumeration remain manual or deferred.

| Tool | Purpose | Finding IDs | Result shape |
|---|---|---|---|

### Parameters

#### `github_check_access`

| Parameter | Kind | Required | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `organization` | string | no | GitHub organization login to assess. Falls back to GITHUB_ORG or GH_ORG. |
| `auth_mode` | string | no | Optional auth mode override. Supported values: pat or app. |
| `api_token` | string | no | Optional GitHub personal access token. Falls back to GITHUB_TOKEN or GH_TOKEN. |
| `app_id` | string | no | Optional GitHub App ID for installation-token auth. |
| `app_private_key` | string | no | Optional PEM private key for GitHub App auth. |
| `app_private_key_path` | string | no | Optional path to a PEM private key file for GitHub App auth. |
| `installation_id` | string or integer | no | Optional GitHub App installation ID for installation-token auth. |
| `enterprise` | string | no | Optional GitHub Enterprise slug for enterprise-level checks. Falls back to GITHUB_ENTERPRISE. |
| `api_base_url` | string | no | Optional GitHub REST API base URL. Defaults to https://api.github.com and can be overridden for GHES-style deployments. Falls back to GITHUB_API_URL or GITHUB_API_BASE_URL. |
| `graphql_url` | string | no | Optional GitHub GraphQL endpoint. Defaults to https://api.github.com/graphql (or HOSTNAME/api/graphql for GHES). Falls back to GITHUB_GRAPHQL_URL. |
| `lookback_days` | integer | no | Optional org audit-log lookback window in days. Defaults to 30. |

## Authentication

Supported modes:

- Fine-grained or classic personal access token
- GitHub App installation token minted from an RS256 JWT

Credential precedence, highest first:

1. Explicit tool arguments
2. GITHUB_TOKEN then GH_TOKEN
3. GitHub App environment or explicit arguments

Environment variables: `GITHUB_TOKEN`, `GH_TOKEN`, `GITHUB_ORG`, `GH_ORG`, `GITHUB_API_URL`, `GITHUB_API_BASE_URL`, `GITHUB_GRAPHQL_URL`, `GITHUB_ENTERPRISE`, `GITHUB_APP_ID`, `GITHUB_APP_PRIVATE_KEY`, `GITHUB_APP_PRIVATE_KEY_PATH`, `GITHUB_APP_INSTALLATION_ID`, `GITHUB_LOOKBACK_DAYS`

Configuration locations: GitHub App private key path supplied explicitly or through GITHUB_APP_PRIVATE_KEY_PATH

Credential and deployment variants: GitHub.com, GitHub Enterprise Server REST /api/v3 and GraphQL /api/graphql origins

Configuration fields: None

Malformed configuration: Reject malformed or ambiguous configuration before any request; never echo credential values.

Credential refresh: POST /app/installations/{installation_id}/access_tokens using a ten-minute GitHub App JWT signed with RS256.

## Rate limits

| Scope | Documented limit | Retry headers | Retryable statuses | Policy |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| GitHub Security Inspector | REST primary and secondary rate limits and GraphQL point limits are account-specific and reported in response headers and rateLimit objects. | `Retry-After`, `X-RateLimit-Remaining`, `X-RateLimit-Reset`, `X-RateLimit-Resource` | 429, 500, 502, 503, 504 | Honor the vendor retry header when present; otherwise retry the declared transient statuses with bounded exponential backoff and jitter, preserving the final failure as unreadable evidence. |

## Checks

### Control coverage

| # | Control | Finding | Verdict semantics |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | SAML SSO is configured and members carry linked identities | GITHUB-ORG-006 | Evaluate the ordered first-match rules for GITHUB-ORG-006 below. |
| 2 | Organization requires 2FA | GITHUB-ORG-001 | Evaluate the ordered first-match rules for GITHUB-ORG-001 below. |
| 3 | Enterprise-managed identity (EMU) governs member accounts | GITHUB-ORG-007 | Evaluate the ordered first-match rules for GITHUB-ORG-007 below. |
| 4 | IP allow list restricts organization access | GITHUB-ORG-008 | Evaluate the ordered first-match rules for GITHUB-ORG-008 below. |
| 5 | Privileged organization access stays limited | GITHUB-ORG-002, GITHUB-ORG-004 | Evaluate the ordered first-match rules for GITHUB-ORG-002, GITHUB-ORG-004 below. |
| 6 | Members cannot create public repositories | GITHUB-ORG-009 | Evaluate the ordered first-match rules for GITHUB-ORG-009 below. |
| 7 | Private repository forking is restricted | GITHUB-ORG-010 | Evaluate the ordered first-match rules for GITHUB-ORG-010 below. |
| 8 | Outside collaborators stay tightly reviewed | GITHUB-ORG-003 | Evaluate the ordered first-match rules for GITHUB-ORG-003 below. |
| 9 | OAuth application access is restricted | GITHUB-INTEG-004 | Evaluate the ordered first-match rules for GITHUB-INTEG-004 below. |
| 10 | Force-push and branch deletion bypass stay restricted | GITHUB-REPO-002, GITHUB-REPO-004 | Evaluate the ordered first-match rules for GITHUB-REPO-002, GITHUB-REPO-004 below. |
| 11 | Default branches require approving pull request reviews | GITHUB-REPO-006 | Evaluate the ordered first-match rules for GITHUB-REPO-006 below. |
| 12 | Default branches require passing status checks | GITHUB-REPO-007 | Evaluate the ordered first-match rules for GITHUB-REPO-007 below. |
| 13 | Web commit signoff is required | GITHUB-REPO-003, GITHUB-REPO-005 | Evaluate the ordered first-match rules for GITHUB-REPO-003, GITHUB-REPO-005 below. |
| 14 | Repositories inherit ruleset-based guardrails | GITHUB-REPO-001 | Evaluate the ordered first-match rules for GITHUB-REPO-001 below. |
| 15 | Code scanning default setup is enabled | GITHUB-CODE-001, GITHUB-CODE-005 | Evaluate the ordered first-match rules for GITHUB-CODE-001, GITHUB-CODE-005 below. |
| 16 | Secret scanning push protection is enabled | GITHUB-CODE-002, GITHUB-CODE-003 | Evaluate the ordered first-match rules for GITHUB-CODE-002, GITHUB-CODE-003 below. |
| 17 | Dependabot and vulnerability defaults are enabled | GITHUB-CODE-004 | Evaluate the ordered first-match rules for GITHUB-CODE-004 below. |
| 18 | Repositories publish a security policy | GITHUB-CODE-006 | Evaluate the ordered first-match rules for GITHUB-CODE-006 below. |
| 19 | Audit log streaming delivers events to an external SIEM | GITHUB-ORG-005, GITHUB-ORG-011 | Evaluate the ordered first-match rules for GITHUB-ORG-005, GITHUB-ORG-011 below. |

### Ordered decision rules

Rules are evaluated from lowest order number to highest. The first matching condition determines the finding status; later rules are not evaluated.

A `matches` condition performs a regular-expression search; anchors are required for whole-value matching, and an `i` flag requests case-insensitive matching. A `ratio` condition divides the numerator by the denominator, applies the declared scale, and rounds to the declared decimal places by choosing the nearest value with exact half cases rounded toward positive infinity; a zero, null, or missing denominator does not match.

| 1 | SAML SSO is configured and members carry linked identities | IA-2, IA-8 | AC.L2-3.1.1 | CC6.1 | 1.1.1 | 8.3.1 | SRG-APP-000148 | ISM-1557 | 5.1.1 |
| 2 | Organization requires 2FA | IA-2(1), IA-2(2) | IA.L2-3.5.3 | CC6.1 | 1.1.2 | 8.4.2 | SRG-APP-000149 | ISM-1401 | 5.1.2 |

| 4 | IP allow list restricts organization access | SC-7, AC-17 | SC.L2-3.13.1 | CC6.1, CC6.6 | 1.2.1 | 1.3.1 | SRG-APP-000142 | ISM-1416 | 5.1.4 |
| 5 | Privileged organization access stays limited | AC-3, AC-6 | AC.L2-3.1.5 | CC6.3 | 1.3.1 | 7.2.1 | SRG-APP-000033 | ISM-1508 | 5.2.1 |
| 6 | Members cannot create public repositories | AC-3, AC-22 | AC.L2-3.1.22 | CC6.1 | 1.3.2 | 7.2.2 | SRG-APP-000211 | ISM-0264 | 5.2.2 |
| 7 | Private repository forking is restricted | AC-3, AC-4 | AC.L2-3.1.3 | CC6.1 | 1.3.3 | 7.2.3 | SRG-APP-000033 | ISM-0405 | 5.2.3 |
| 8 | Outside collaborators stay tightly reviewed | AC-2, AC-6(5) | AC.L2-3.1.6 | CC6.2, CC6.3 | 1.3.4 | 7.2.4 | SRG-APP-000340 | ISM-1509 | 5.2.4 |

| 24 | GitHub App installations hold least-privilege permissions | AC-6(10), CM-11 | AC.L2-3.1.7 | CC6.3, CC6.8 | 5.2.2 | 6.3.2 | SRG-APP-000342 | ISM-1490 | 5.6.4 |
| 25 | Package registry visibility is governed | AC-3, AC-22 | AC.L2-3.1.22 | CC6.1 | 5.3.1 | 7.2.6 | SRG-APP-000211 | ISM-0264 | 5.6.5 |

## Collection states

| State | Required rendering |
|---|---|
| complete | complete: proven API exhaustion or a successful single-object read. |
| truncated | truncated: preserve seen and total when available plus the exact stop reason. |
| unreadable | unreadable: render data and counts as null and retain a scrubbed error envelope. |
| denied | denied: render null evidence with the endpoint and HTTP status, never an empty inventory. |
| not requested | not_requested: identify the unreadable parent dependency and do not invent an HTTP status. |
| not configured | not_configured: identify the absent optional feature or credential without treating it as compliant. |

## Integration-specific scrubbing

Shared contract version: 1.1.

Projection stage: Project records to verdict-consumed fields, scrub configured and discovered credentials, then scrub again at every report and archive write sink.

Sensitive fields and values: token, private_key, authorization, cookie, config.secret, openssh_public_key, email, url, query

Credential formats: GitHub PAT prefixes, GitHub App installation tokens, PEM private keys, Bearer and Basic headers, webhook secrets

Reviewed benign exceptions: Stable non-secret resource identifiers and public documentation URLs remain visible unless carried in a credential field.

Integration-specific rules:

- Withhold undocumented error bodies; retain only status, media type, byte length, and allowlisted vendor error codes.
- Remove URL user information, queries, and fragments from evidence and reject off-origin pagination links.
- Unavailable counts, arrays, maps, and negative flags are null rather than fabricated empty values.

Projected fields by surface:

| Surface | Allowed fields |
|---|---|
| `org-access` | `two_factor_requirement_enabled`, `default_repository_permission`, `members_can_create_public_repositories`, `members_can_fork_private_repositories`, `login`, `role`, `samlIdentityProvider`, `externalIdentities`, `ipAllowListEntries` |
| `repo-protection` | `default_branch`, `archived`, `rules`, `required_pull_request_reviews`, `required_status_checks`, `required_signatures`, `allow_force_pushes`, `allow_deletions` |
| `actions-security` | `enabled_repositories`, `allowed_actions`, `default_workflow_permissions`, `can_approve_pull_request_reviews`, `visibility`, `selected_repositories_url` |
| `code-security` | `name`, `enforcement`, `code_scanning_default_setup`, `secret_scanning`, `secret_scanning_push_protection`, `dependabot_alerts`, `dependabot_security_updates` |
| `integrations` | `active`, `config.url`, `config.insecure_ssl`, `config.secret`, `permissions`, `repository_selection`, `suspended_at`, `read_only`, `created_at` |

|---|---|---|---|---|
| `README.md` | markdown | Always. | The runtime-generated bundle metadata or operator guidance. | UTF-8 text. |
| `QUICK_REFERENCE.md` | markdown | Always. | The runtime-generated bundle metadata or operator guidance. | UTF-8 text. |
| `metadata.json` | json | Always. | The runtime-generated bundle metadata or operator guidance. | UTF-8 JSON with two-space indentation and a trailing newline. |
| `core_data/access_check.json` | json | Always. | The projected runtime dataset or its explicit unavailable marker. | UTF-8 JSON with two-space indentation and a trailing newline. |
| `analysis/findings.json` | json | Always. | Runtime assessment or finding records. | UTF-8 JSON with two-space indentation and a trailing newline. |
| `analysis/org_access.json` | json | Always. | Runtime assessment or finding records. | UTF-8 JSON with two-space indentation and a trailing newline. |
| `analysis/repo_protection.json` | json | Always. | Runtime assessment or finding records. | UTF-8 JSON with two-space indentation and a trailing newline. |
| `analysis/actions_security.json` | json | Always. | Runtime assessment or finding records. | UTF-8 JSON with two-space indentation and a trailing newline. |
| `analysis/code_security.json` | json | Always. | Runtime assessment or finding records. | UTF-8 JSON with two-space indentation and a trailing newline. |
| `analysis/integrations.json` | json | Always. | Runtime assessment or finding records. | UTF-8 JSON with two-space indentation and a trailing newline. |
| `compliance/executive_summary.md` | markdown | Always. | The runtime-generated human-readable compliance report. | UTF-8 text. |
| `compliance/unified_compliance_matrix.md` | markdown | Always. | The runtime-generated human-readable compliance report. | UTF-8 text. |

| `_errors.log` | text | Written when any access probe, assessment dataset, child lookup, SQL statement, or archive step reports a collection error. | The runtime-generated bundle metadata or operator guidance. | UTF-8 text. |

### Record schemas

#### finding

- `id`
- `title`
- `severity`
- `status`
- `summary`
- `evidence`
- `framework mappings`

#### collection_marker

- `collected`
- `status`
- `endpoint`
- `error`

#### bundle_result

- `outputDir`
- `zipPath`
- `fileCount`
- `findingCount`
- `errorCount`

#### assessment

- `title or category`
- `summary`
- `findings`
- `errors when collection was partial`

#### pagination_state

- `items or rows seen`
- `reported total when available`
- `pages`
- `truncated`
- `stop reason`

JSON formatting: UTF-8 JSON with two-space indentation and a trailing newline.

Overwrite policy: Allocate github-<organization>-audit-<UTC timestamp> and append a numeric suffix while either paired path exists.

Path safety: Resolve beneath the configured output root and reject traversal, unsafe parents, files, and symbolic-link escapes.

Archive pairing: Create <allocated-directory>.zip beside the GitHub audit directory with the identical suffix.
